Use the tools to inspect the code and make your changes; a response without a tool call ends the session, so only reply without one when you are done.\n\nInspect completed evaluation reports, generation metadata, patches, and relevant failure logs before proposing a change. Compare each candidate with its parent and the initial agent; the newest generation is not necessarily the best, and an unevaluated generation is not evidence of improvement. Preserve behavior from the strongest completed result unless there is a specific, evidence-based reason to change it. Treat failed cases as clues for hypotheses, not as justification for unsupported task-specific hints. Prefer a small general change that addresses an identifiable failure mode while preserving solved cases, and keep agent interfaces and enforced budgets intact. Do not claim improvement unless evaluation confirms it."

genid, skip_staged_eval=False, num_samples=-1):\n    # NOTE: the harness for polyglot is different because each task instance needs a docker container\n    from domains.polyglot.harness import harness as harness_polyglot\n    from domains.polyglot.report import report as report_polyglot\n\n    eval_output_dir = os.path.join(output_dir, f"gen_{genid}", "polyglot_eval")\n    test_more_threshold = 0.4  # NOTE: same setting as that in DGM\n    model_name_or_path = "eval_run"\n    patch_files = get_patch_files(output_dir, genid)\n    run_next_eval = True\n\n    # Small sample size evaluation for staged eval\n    if not skip_staged_eval:\n        test_task_list = load_json_file("./domains/polyglot/subsets/small.json")\n        dnames = harness_polyglot(\n            test_task_list=test_task_list,\n            num_samples=-1,\n            max_workers=10,\n            model_name_or_path=model_name_or_path,\n            model_patch_paths=patch_files,\n            num_evals=1,\n            num_evals_parallel=1,\n            pred_dname=eval_output_dir,\n            output_dir=eval_output_dir,\n            root_dir=root_dir,\n        )\n        report_polyglot(output_dir=eval_output_dir, run_keyword=model_name_or_path, expected_num_tasks=len(test_task_list))\n        stagedeval_score = get_score("polyglot", output_dir, genid)\n        run_next_eval = stagedeval_score is not None and stagedeval_score >= test_more_threshold\n\n    # Check if additional evaluation should be run\n    if run_next_eval:\n        test_task_list_more = load_json_file("./domains/polyglot/subsets/medium.json")\n        dnames = harness_polyglot(\n            test_task_list=test_task_list + test_task_list_more,\n            num_samples=num_samples,\n            max_workers=10,\n            model_name_or_path=model_name_or_path,\n            model_patch_paths=patch_files,\n            num_evals=1,\n            num_evals_parallel=1,\n            pred_dname=eval_output_dir,\n            output_dir=eval_output_dir,\n            root_dir=root_dir,\n        )\n        report_polyglot(output_dir=eval_output_dir, run_keyword=model_name_or_path, expected_num_tasks=len(test_task_list + test_task_list_more))\n\n    # Update metadata\n    update_node_metadata(output_dir, genid, {"run_full_eval": run_next_eval})\n\ndef select_next_parent_container(\n    docker_client,\n    domains,\n    generate_output_dir,\n    archive,\n    root_dir="./",\n    root_commit="HEAD",\n    max_attempts=10,\n):\n    # Setup logger\n    logger = setup_logger(os.path.join(generate_output_dir, "select_next_parent.log"))\n\n    # Get the latest node that can select parent\n    latest_node = get_latest_can_select_parent(archive, generate_output_dir)\n    safe_log(f"select_next_parent_container: latest_node={latest_node}")\n    prev_patch_files = get_patch_files(generate_output_dir, latest_node)\n\n    # Create and start the Docker container\n    image_name = f"{REPO_NAME}"\n    run_id = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")\n    container_name = f"{REPO_NAME}-nextparent-container-{run_id}"\n    container = build_container(docker_client, root_dir, image_name, container_name, verbose=False)\n    container.start()\n    container_output_folder = "/tmp/"\n\n    try:\n        # Apply all lineage diffs\n        commit_hash = apply_diffs_container(container, prev_patch_files, verbose=False)\n\n        # Copy generate_output_dir to container\n        container_generate_output_dir = os.path.join(\n            container_output_folder, generate_output_dir.split(os.sep)[-1]\n        )\n        copy_to_container(\n            container,\n            source_path=generate_output_dir,\n            dest_path=container_generate_output_dir,\n            verbose=False,\n        )\n\n        # Get next parent in container\n        command = [\n            "timeout",\n            "3600",  # 1h timeout\n            "python",\n            "-m",\n            "utils.run_select_next_parent",\n            "--domains",\n            *domains,\n            "--generate_output_dir",\n            container_generate_output_dir,\n        ]\n        exec_result = container.exec_run(cmd=command, workdir=f"/{REPO_NAME}")\n        log_container_output(exec_result, verbose=True)\n\n        # Get next parent outputs\n        container_output_strings = exec_result.output.decode().strip().split("\\n")\n        next_parent_genid = container_output_strings[-1]\n        next_parent_genid = int(next_parent_genid) if not is_starting_node(next_parent_genid) else next_parent_genid\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        safe_log(f"Error in select_next_parent_container: {e}")\n c.request("POST", "/v1/chat", body=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})\n            r = c.getresponse()\n            data = json.loads(r.read() or b"{}")\n            c.close()\n Preserve the demonstrated grid shape and colors unless the examples establish a change.",\n        "",\n    ]\n    for i, pair in enumerate(inputs["train"], 1):\n        parts.append(f"## Example {i}")\n        h, w = len(pair["input"]), len(pair["input"][0])\n        parts.append(f"Input ({h}x{w}):")\n        parts.append(grid_to_text(pair["input"]))\n        h, w = len(pair["output"]), len(pair["output"][0])\n        parts.append(f"Output ({h}x{w}):")\n        parts.append(grid_to_text(pair["output"]))\n        parts.append("")\n    t = inputs["test_input"]\n    parts.append("## Test")\n    parts.append(f"Input ({len(t)}x{len(t[0])}):")\n    parts.append(grid_to_text(t))\n    parts.append("")\n    parts.append(\n        "Give the output grid for the test input. r in g}) == 1\n            and all(isinstance(v, int) and not isinstance(v, bool) and 0 <= v <= 9 for r in g for v in r))\n\n\ndef _parse_block(s):\n    s = s.strip()\n    try:\n        g = json.loads(s)\n        if _valid(g):\n            return g\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    # rows of digits, optionally space/comma separated\n    rows = []\n    for line in s.splitlines():\n        line = line.strip().strip("[](),")\n        if not line:\n            continue\n        toks = re.findall(r"\\d", line) if re.fullmatch(r"[\\d\\s,]+", line) else None\n        if not toks:\n            return None\n        rows.append([int(x) for x in toks])\n    return rows if _valid(rows) else None\n\n\ndef parse_grid(text):\n    """Extract the answer grid from a model path\nsys.path.append(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "../.."))\n\nimport argparse\nimport importlib.util\nimport json\nimport pandas as pd\n\n\ndef report(\n    dname,\n    domain,\n    suffix="",  # suffix to the predictions{suffix}.csv filename\n):\n    # Dynamically import functions based on the domain\n    utils_prefix = domain.split("_", 1)[1] + "_" if domain.startswith("imo_") else ""\n    domain_folder = domain.split(\'_\')[0] if "imo_" in domain else domain\n    utils_module_path = f"domains.{domain_folder}.{utils_prefix}utils"\n    utils_module = importlib.import_module(utils_module_path)\n    ground_truth_key = utils_module.GROUND_TRUTH_KEY\n    question_id_col = utils_module.QUESTION_ID\n\n    # Load and process the data\n    path = os.path.join(dname, f"predictions{suffix}.csv")\n    df = pd.read_csv(path, dtype=str)\n    df = df[df["prediction"] != ""].copy()  # Filter out rows with NA predictions\n    df["prediction"] = df["prediction"].str.strip().str.lower()\n    df[ground_truth_key] = df[ground_truth_key].str.strip().str.lower()\n    df["match"] = df[ground_truth_key] == df["prediction"]\n\n    # Calculate overall accuracy\n    accuracy = df["match"].mean()\n    total_correct = int(df["match"].sum())\n    total = len(df)\n    print(f"Accuracy: {accuracy:.3f}, Total correct: {total_correct} / {total}")\n\n    # Calculate Mean Absolute Error (MAE)\n    if domain == "imo_grading":\n        try:\n            max_error = 7\n            df["prediction_points"] = (\n                df["prediction"]\n                .map({"incorrect": 0, "partial": 1, "almost": 6, "correct": 7})\n                .astype(float)\n            )\n            df[\'reward_points\'] = (\n                df[ground_truth_key]\n                .map({"incorrect": 0, "partial": 1, "almost": 6, "correct": 7})\n                .astype(float)\n            )\n            df["error"] = abs(df["prediction_points"] - df[\'reward_points\'])\n            df["error"] = df["error"].fillna(max_error)\n            mae = df["error"].mean() / max_error\n            print(f"Normalized Mean Absolute Error (MAE): {mae:.3f}")\n        except Exception as e:\n            mae = None\n            print("Error: Could not calculate MAE for IMO grading")\n            print(e)\n\n    # Accuracy by label\n    label_accuracies = df.groupby(ground_truth_key)["match"].mean()\n    label_counts = df[ground_truth_key].value_counts()\n\n    print("\\nAccuracy by label:")\n    label_report = {}\n    labels = set(df[ground_truth_key].unique())\n    for label in labels:\n        # True Positive (TP): predicted = label and actual = label\n        tp = ((df["prediction"] == label) & (df[ground_truth_key] == label)).sum()\n        # False Positive (FP): predicted = label but actual ≠ label\n        fp = ((df["prediction"] == label) & (df[ground_truth_key] != label)).sum()\n        # False Negative (FN): predicted ≠ label but actual = label\n        fn = ((df["prediction"] != label) & (df[ground_truth_key] == label)).sum()\n        # Precision and Recall\n        precision = tp / (tp + fp) if (tp + fp) > 0 else 0.0\n        recall = tp / (tp + fn) if (tp + fn) > 0 else 0.0\n        correct_label = tp\n        total_label = int(label_counts.get(label, 0))\n\n        print(\n            f"  Label: {label} - Precision: {precision:.3f}, Recall: {recall:.3f}, Correct: {correct_label} / {total_label}"\n        )\n        label_report[str(label)] = {\n            "precision": float(precision),\n            "recall": float(recall),\n            "correct": int(correct_label),\n            "total": int(total_label),\n        }\n\n    # Compute label distributions for winner and prediction\n    winner_distribution = df[ground_truth_key].value_counts(normalize=True).to_dict()\n    prediction_distribution = df["prediction"].value_counts(normalize=True).to_dict()\n\n    print("\\nDistribution of ground truth labels:")\n    for label, freq in winner_distribution.items():\n        print(f"  {label}: {freq:.3f}")\n\n    print("\\nDistribution of prediction labels:")\n    for label, freq in prediction_distribution.items():\n        print(f"  {label}: {freq:.3f}")\n\n    # Compute expected random guess accuracy using winner label distribution\n    random_guess_accuracy = sum(p**2 for p in winner_distribution.values())\n    print(\n        f"\\nExpected random guess accuracy (based on ground_truth_key label distribution): {random_guess_accuracy:.3f}"\n    )\n\n    # Build the report dictionary\n    report = {\n        "overall_accuracy": float(accuracy),\n        **({"normalized_mean_absolute_error": mae} if domain == "imo_grading" else {}),\n        "total_correct": total_correct,\n        "total": total,\n        "accuracy_by_ground_truth": label_report,\n        "label_distribution": {\n            "ground_truth": winner_distribution,\n            "prediction": prediction_distribution,\n        },\n        "random_guess_accuracy": random_guess_accuracy,\n        "question_ids_failed": [\n            row[question_id_col] for _, row in df[df["match"] == False].iterrows()\n        ],\n        "question_ids_passed": [\n            row[question_id_col] for _, row in df[df["match"] == True].iterrows()\n        ],\n    }\n\n    # Save the report as a JSON file\n    report_path = os.path.join(dname, f"report{suffix}.json")\n    with open(report_path, "w") as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=4)\n\n    return report, report_path\n\ndef _UnixConn(http.client.HTTPConnection):\\n    def __init__(self, path, timeout=3600):\\n        super().__init__("localhost", timeout=timeout)\\n        self._path = path\\n\\n    def connect(self):\\n        s = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM)\\n        s.settimeout(self.timeout)\\n        s.connect(self._path)\\n        self.sock = s\\n\\n\\ndef call_model(prompt):\\n    """One model call through the budget gateway (model and reasoning effort are fixed there)."""\\n    path = os.environ.get("GW_SOCKET", "/run/arcgw/gw.sock")\\n    body = json.dumps({"token": os.environ.get("GW_TOKEN", ""),\\n                       "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}]}).encode()\\n    last = None\\n    for attempt in range(3):\\n        try:\\n            c = _UnixConn(path)\\n            c.request("POST", "/v1/chat", body=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})\\n            r = c.getresponse()\\n            data = json.loads(r.read() or b"{}")\\n            c.close()\\n            if r.status == 200:\\n                return data.get("text", "")\\n            last = f"gateway HTTP {r.status}: {data.get(\\\'error\\\')}"\\n            if r.status in (402, 403, 429):\\n                break\\n        except Exception as e:\\n            last = f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"\\n        time.sleep(5 * (attempt + 1))\\n    raise RuntimeError(last)\\n\\n\\ndef grid_to_text(grid):\\n    return "\\\\n".join(" ".join(str(v) for v in row) for row in grid)\\n\\n\\ndef build_prompt(inputs):\\n    parts = [\\n        "You are solving an ARC-AGI puzzle. Each grid is a rectangular 2D array of integers 0-9 "\\n        "(each integer is a color). The demonstration pairs below share one hidden transformation rule "\\n        "that maps each input grid to its output grid. Infer the rule and apply it to the test input.",\\n        "",\\n    ]\\n    for i, pair in enumerate(inputs["train"], 1):\\n        parts.append(f"## Example {i}")\\n        h, w = len(pair["input"]), len(pair["input"][0])\\n        parts.append(f"Input ({h}x{w}):")\\n        parts.append(grid_to_text(pair["input"]))\\n        h, w = len(pair["output"]), len(pair["output"][0])\\n        parts.append(f"Output ({h}x{w}):")\\n        parts.append(grid_to_text(pair["output"]))\\n        parts.append("")\\n    t = inputs["test_input"]\\n    parts.append("## Test")\\n    parts.append(f"Input ({len(t)}x{len(t[0])}):")\\n    parts.append(grid_to_text(t))\\n    parts.append("")\\n    parts.append(\\n        "Give the output grid for the test input. End your response with the grid inside <answer></answer> "\\n        "tags as a JSON list of rows, for example:\\\\n<answer>[[0, 1, 2], [3, 4, 5]]</answer>\\\\n"\\n        "Put nothing else inside the tags."\\n    )\\n    return "\\\\n".join(parts)\\n\\n\\ndef _valid(g):\\n    return (isinstance(g, list) and len(g) > 0 and all(isinstance(r, list) and len(r) > 0 for r in g)\\n            and len({len(r) for r in g}) == 1\\n            and all(isinstance(v, int) and not isinstance(v, bool) and 0 <= v <= 9 for r in g for v in r))\\n\\n\\ndef _parse_block(s):\\n    s = s.strip()\\n    try:\\n        g = json.loads(s)\\n        if _valid(g):\\n            return g\\n    except Exception:\\n        pass\\n    # rows of digits, optionally space/comma separated\\n    rows = []\\n    for line in s.splitlines():\\n        line = line.strip().strip("[](),")\\n        if not line:\\n            continue\\n        toks = re.findall(r"\\\\d", line) if re.fullmatch(r"[\\\\d\\\\s,]+", line) else None\\n        if not toks:\\n            return None\\n        rows.append([int(x) for x in toks])\\n    return rows if _valid(rows) else None\\n\\n\\ndef parse_grid(text):\\n    """Extract the answer grid from a model response; None if no valid grid is found."""\\n    if not text:\\n        return None\\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"<answer>(.*?)</answer>", text, re.DOTALL)):\\n        g = _parse_block(m)\\n        if g is not None:\\n            return g\\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"```(?:json)?\\\\s*(.*?)```", text, re.DOTALL)):\\n        g = _parse_block(m)\\n        if g is not None:\\n            return g\\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"\\\\[\\\\s*\\\\[[\\\\d\\\\s,\\\\[\\\\]]*\\\\]\\\\s*\\\\]", text)):\\n        g = f.write(msg + "\\\\n\\\\n")\\n        except Exception:\\n            pass\\n\\n    def solve_once(self, inputs):\\n        """One direct model response -> one parsed grid (or None)."""\\n forward(self, inputs):\\n        """\\n        Args:\\n            inputs (dict): {"domain": "arc", "train": [{"input": grid, "output": grid}, ...], "test_input": grid}\\n        Returns:\\n            tuple: (prediction, msg_history) where prediction is a list of up to 2 candidate grids.\\n        """\\n        answers = []\\n        for _ in range(2):\\n            try:\\n                g = commit_hash = apply_diffs_container(container, prev_patch_files)\\n\\n        # Copy generate_output_dir to container\\n        container_generate_output_dir = os.path.join(\\n            container_output_folder, generate_output_dir.split(os.sep)[-1]\\n        )\\n        copy_to_container(\\n            container,\\n            source_path=generate_output_dir,\\n            dest_path=container_generate_output_dir,\\n        )\\n\\n        # Get ensemble scores in container\\n        scores = []\\n        for subset in subsets:\\n            command = [\\n                "timeout",\\n                "10800",  # 3h timeout\\n                "python",\\n                "-m",\\n                "utils.run_ensemble",\\n                "--domain",\\n                domain,\\n                "--generate_output_dir",\\n                container_generate_output_dir,\\n                "--num_samples",\\n                str(num_samples),\\n                "--max_workers",\\n                str(max_workers),\\n                "--subset",\\n                subset,\\n            ]\\n            exec_result = container.exec_run(cmd=command, workdir=f"/{REPO_NAME}")\\n            log_container_output(exec_result)\\n\\n            # Get ensemble outputs\\n            container_output_strings = exec_result.output.decode().strip().split("\\\\n")\\n            score = float(container_output_strings[-3])\\n            scores.append(score)\\n--- initial chat endings and error indicators
